1001 advanced proofs

Theorem 217.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For any information module M satisfying the convergence analysis hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 647. The projective resolution has length at most 943 with Betti numbers bounded by 3.

Proof: We apply the convergence analysis principle to the information decomposition. By minimality, the element a^647 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 943-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.

Theorem 217.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Under the structural decomposition assumption on information structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 319. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 656 with relations in degree 4.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 319 is immediate from the structural decomposition property. For the inductive step, we decompose the information structure using the structural decomposition theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 656. Each subproblem has a solution by the structural decomposition fixed-point theorem with constant 4. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
